Coordinate line search performance in numpy:
Function `line_search_coord(i, a, C, full_sweeps?)`:
```
old = a[i]

Now, the exact coordinate line search can be applied to the objective after normalization but uses current C. It directly optimizes F exactly (not smoothed). This may be the most important. Sequence of steps:
- Start from parent normalized sum=1. Compute C. Evaluate score.
- Phase 0: Coordinate polishing full sweeps (time maybe 300s): Use top-ordered by gradient, exact line search; repeat until no improvement or time. Since parent already good, this may extract quick improvements.
- Phase 1: Smooth L-BFGS/Adam with several μ to move to better basin. After each, coordinate polish.
- Phase 2: More coordinate polish and maybe block moves.
- Phase 3: Random perturbations of current best (noise in θ) then coordinate polish, to escape local minima.
- Final: Return best sequence (sum 1, clipped, finite).

       e = np.exp(th)
       s=e.sum()
       if s<=0: return np.full(len(theta),1/len(theta))
       return e/s
   ```
- `a_to_theta(a, n)`: `a_safe=np.maximum(a,1e-300); th=np.log(a_safe); th -= np.mean(th); return th`
- `fft_conv_self(a)`: fsize=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length; A=fft(a,fsize); c=real(ifft(A*A)); return c[:2n-1]
  - For small n maybe direct? Use `np.convolve` if n<128? not necessary.
- `gradient_weighted(W, a)`: as derived: `ar=a[::-1]`; fsize=1<<(3*n-2).bit_length(); Wp length L, Arp length n. conv = real(ifft(fft(Wp,fsize)*fft(Arp,fsize))); return 2*conv[n-1:n-1+n]
  - Need ensure W length L. If W not symmetric no issue. Use `np.ascontiguousarray`.
- `smooth_weights(C, mu)`: `z=C/mu`; z-=max; w=exp(z); w/=w.sum(); w=0.5*(w+w[::-1]) maybe; w/=w.sum(); return w

Need if C all zero? return 0, zeros.
  - But value doesn't depend on scores scaled? fine.

def coord_update_one(i, a, C, f, tmp, tol):
    n=len(a); L=len(C)
    old=a[i]
    Cseg=C[i:i+n]
    outside_left = C[:i].max() if i>0 else -inf
    outside_right = C[i+n:].max() if i+n<L else -inf
    outside = max(outside_left, outside_right)
    best_val=f; best_delta=0.0
    if old > 1e-15:
       logfs = ...
       deltas = old*(np.exp(logfs)-1)
    else:
       scale = max(a.max(),1e-6)*? maybe precomputed maxa
       deltas = scale * [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0]
    for delta in deltas: if delta==0 continue; val = eval; if val<best_val: best...
    # refine if best_delta !=0 and best_val < f - tol?
    if old>1e-15 and best_delta !=0 and best_val < f - 1e-16:
       best_log = log(best_delta/old +1)
       refine_ls = [ -0.05,-0.02,-0.01,0.01,0.02,0.05, -0.1,0.1]
       for h in refine:
           tau=exp(best_log+h)
           if tau<=0: continue
           delta=old*(tau-1)
           val=eval
           if val<best_val: best...
    if best_val < f - tol:
       # apply
       delta=best_delta
       C[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a
       C[2*i] += delta*delta
       s=1+delta
       a = a / s? Need modify in place. In Python, a[scale] returns new; to update current array in place:
       a *= (1.0/s) ??? But a[i] should be old+s delta? Let's do:
       a[i] = old + delta
       a *= 1.0/s
       C *= 1.0/(s*s)
       return best_val, True
    return f, False
```

def block_update_once(a, C, f, K, rng, tol, tmpL=None):
    n=len(a); L=2n-1
    # compute gradient order top K
    mu = f/20 focus? weights
    ga = gradient_weighted(w,a)
    gth = a*(ga-dot)
    idx = rng.choice? top K by abs
    if len<2 return
    d = np.zeros(n)
    vals = gth[idx]

    d[idx] = -vals / (np.max(np.abs(vals)) + 1e-15)
    # Maybe remove mean? not needed.
    # t range:
    pos = d>0; neg=d<0
    t_hi = np.inf; if any pos: t_hi = min(a[pos]/d[pos])
    t_lo = -np.inf; if any neg: t_lo = max(a[neg]/(-d[neg]))
    t_max = min(t_hi, 0.5)?? If t_hi huge, scale by 0.5*maxa/maxd? Use 0.2 maybe.
    t_min = max(t_lo, -0.2)
    scale_t = 0.2 / (np.max(np.abs(d)) + 1e-15)
    candidates = [0, 0.25,0.5,1.0,-0.5,-1?]*scale_t within [t_min,t_max]
    # compute ad, dd once
    ad=np.zeros(L)
    for j in nz: ad[j:j+n] += d[j]*a
    dd=np.zeros(L)
    for j in nz:

    s_sum = d.sum()
    # evaluate candidates:

       if abs(denom)<1e-12 skip

       a += t*d; s=1+t*s_sum; a /= s; np.add(C,2t ad,out=C)? modify C in place: C += 2t*ad + t²*dd; C /= s²;
       # due to negative zeros, a = maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max() (safe)

```
def block_candidate_value(t, C, ad, dd, sumd, tmpL):
    denom=1+t*sumd
    if denom<=0: return inf
    np.add(C, 2.0*t*ad, out=tmpL)
    tmpL += t*t*dd
    val=np.max(tmpL)/(denom*denom)
    return val
```

Now, root initialization:
Implement `sqrt_root_init(n, rng=None)`:
```
def sqrt_root_init(n):
   L=2*n-1
   c=L
   a=np.zeros(n)
   a[0]=1.0/np.sqrt(L)
   for k in range(1,n):
       s=0.0
       # sum i=0..k-1 a[i]a[k-i], both <n since k<n
       for i in range(k): s += a[i]*a[k-i]
       b=2.0*s
       target=1.0/L
       disc=b*b + target
       a[k]=(-b + np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
   a = np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-15: return ones
   a/=s
   # Also try reversed and centered? Maybe return best of a, a[::-1], maybe mix? We can evaluate all.
   return a
```
Loop O(n²) for n=4096 ~8M Python loops, too slow! Need vectorize recurrence? k up to 4095, inner sum increasing -> ~8M Python operations; maybe 0.5s? 8M Python ~0.5-1s, acceptable maybe. But we can avoid or use `np.dot` per k (slower due to overhead). 4096 dotos average length 2048 -> 8M C ops + 4096 Python calls, okay maybe 0.1s? Use `s = np.dot(a[:k], a[k-1::-1])` (sum a[i]*a[k-i]). That creates slices; overhead 4096, okay. Let's do vectorized per k:
```
for k in range(1,n):
    s = np.dot(a[:k], a[k-1::-1])
```
Total memory small. 4096 dotos maybe 0.2s. Good.
But the recurrence target uniform `1/L` for k up to n-1; is it stable? sqrt. We'll evaluate. To save time, maybe only if n <= 8192. n=4096 okay.
Also try `a = 0.5*a +0.5*a[::-1]` (symmetrize) and normalize; evaluate.

Implement `make_pref_suf(C)`:
```
def make_pref_suf(C):
    L=C.size
    pref=np.empty(L+1); pref[0]=-np.inf; pref[1:]=np.maximum.accumulate(C)
    suf=np.empty(L+1); suf[L]=-np.inf; suf[:L]=np.maximum.accumulate(C[::-1])[::-1]
    return pref,suf
```
Check suf[k]=max(C[k:]). For k=L -inf. For idx+n maybe L, use -inf.

`coord_polish`:
```
def coord_polish(a, C, f, time_budget, top_k=None, focus=20, tol=1e-14):
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
   a=a.copy(); C=C.copy(); tmp=np.empty(len(a))
   best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_f=f
   sweep=0
   while time<deadline:
      sweep+=1
      # recompute C/f? It should be consistent; do every loop maybe.
      s=a.sum(); if s<=0 break; a=a/s? Wait a sum 1. If sum drift, normalize and C scale. But if we do a = a/s; C=C/(s*s); f=C.max()
      if f<best_f: update best
      # compute order
      mu=f/focus; w=smooth_weights(C, mu)
      ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
      dot=np.dot(a,ga)
      gth=a*(ga-dot)
      if top_k is None:
         order=np.argsort(-np.abs(gth))
      else:
         order=np.argsort(-np.abs(gth))[:top_k]
      pref,suf=make_pref_suf(C)
      add_scale=max(a.max(),1e-8)
      improved_sweep=False
      for pos, idx in enumerate(order):
         if time>deadline-1: break
         new_f, improved = coord_update_one(a,C,f,idx,pref,suf,tmp,tol,old? add_scale)
         if improved:
            f=new_f; improved_sweep=True
            # recompute pref/suf, add_scale
            pref,suf=make_pref_suf(C)
            add_scale=max(a.max(),1e-8)
            if f < best_f - tol:
               best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
      # end sweep: recompute normalized C to correct
      s=a.sum(); a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=s; C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
      if f < best_f - tol:
          best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
      if not improved_sweep:
          break
      if sweep >= 200: break # safety
   return best_a,best_C,best_f
```

But `coord_update_one` needs `f` current before candidate. If within sweep after prior improvements, f updated. Good.

`smooth_weights`:
```
def smooth_weights(C, mu):
   if mu <= 0: return max idx delta
   z=C/mu; z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: zero max; else w/=s; w=0.5*(w+w[::-1]); w/=w.sum(); return w
```
For mu extremely small, z max 0; underflow others; w delta at max. `smooth_weights` stable.

def run_adam_theta(a, time_budget, focus_start=5, focus_end=200, lr_start=0.1, lr_end=0.03):
   a=a.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()

   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0

   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
   no_imp=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
   while time<deadline:
      a=theta_to_a(theta)
      C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
      if f<best_f - 1e-15: best... no_imp=0 else no_imp+=1
      if no_imp > 1000:
          # restart theta around best with noise
          th_best=a_to_theta(best_a)
          noise=0.02*(0.5)
          theta=th_best + noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
          m=v=0
          no_imp=0
          continue
      progress=...
      if progress<0.5: focus=focus_start+(focus_end/4? maybe) etc.
      mu=max(f/focus,1e-15)
      w=smooth_weights(C,mu)
      ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
      dot=np.dot(a,ga)
      gth=a*(ga-dot)
      t+=1; m=...; lr=lr_start*(lr_end/lr_start)**progress maybe
      theta = theta - lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
      theta -= theta.mean()
      # Optional threshold? no
   # after loop, return best_a, best_f
```

   theta -= theta.mean()

`block_update_once` details with gradient ordering. To avoid computing gradient each time from scratch? Use pass from block_polish. Simpler inside.
```
def block_update_once(a,C,f,tmpL,rng,K,focus=20,tol=1e-14):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   mu=max(f/focus,1e-15)
   w=smooth_weights(C,mu)
   ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
   dot=np.dot(a,ga)
   gth=a*(ga-dot)
   scores=np.abs(gth)
   # top K by scores; include a few random if sparse
   top=np.argpartition(scores, K)[:K] maybe; or argsort.
   # maybe shuffle top
   nz_d = np.abs(gth[top])
   if nz_d.max() < 1e-18: return a,C,f,False
   d=np.zeros(n)
   scale = nz_d.max()
   d[top] = -gth[top] / scale

Detailed code for helpers:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAVE_LBFGS = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LBFGS = False

_LB = {'mu': None, 'count': 0}

def theta_to_a(theta):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    th = th - np.max(th)
    e = np.exp(th)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(th.shape, 1.0/len(th), dtype=np.float64)
    return e/s
```
If theta has inf? If L-BFGS drifts to inf, max inf, th nan. Need handle: if not all finite, fallback uniform or clip.
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): return uniform
th = np.nan_to_num(th, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
Include.

`a_to_theta`:
```
def a_to_theta(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return zeros
   a=a/s
   a=np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
   th=np.log(a)
   th -= th.mean()
   return th
```

`fft_conv_self`:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size; size=2*n-1; fsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   A=fft(a, fsize)
   c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:size]
   # clean small negatives
   c = np.where(c < 0.0, np.maximum(c, 0.0), c) # this cleans all negatives to 0
   return c
```
`np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)` modifies.

`gradient_weighted`:
```
def gradient_weighted(W, a):
   n=a.size; L=W.size
   ar=a[::-1]
   fsize=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()
   conv=np.real(ifft(fft(W, fsize)*fft(ar, fsize)))
   g=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
   return 2.0*g
```
Check fsize bit: `L+n-2` = (2n-1)+n-2=3n-3; length max 3n-2. `1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()`? For size required N, next power >=N: `1 << (N-1).bit_length()`. N=L+n-1=3n-2. Use `N = L+n-1; fsize=1 << (N-1).bit_length()`. Good.
- `W` could not be contiguous; okay.

`smooth_weights`:
```
def smooth_weights(C, mu):
   L=C.size; w=np.zeros(L)
   if C.size==0: return w
   f=C.max()
   if mu <= 1e-300 or f <= 1e-300:
      w[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0; return w
   z=C/mu
   zmax=z.max(); z=z-zmax
   w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300:
      w[:] = 0; w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
   else:
      w /= s
   w = 0.5*(w + w[::-1])
   s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
   return w
```
If C has max many, okay. zmax can be inf if mu tiny? C finite. If z values >709, exp underflow; z-zmax <=0 so exp <=1. Good.

`make_pref_suf`:
```
def make_pref_suf(C):
   L=C.size
   pref=np.empty(L+1); pref[0]=-np.inf; pref[1:]=np.maximum.accumulate(C)
   suf=np.empty(L+1); suf[L]=-np.inf
   rmax=np.maximum.accumulate(C[::-1])
   suf[:L]=rmax[::-1]
   return pref,suf
```
If C contains nan? no.

`coord_candidate_eval`:
```
def coord_candidate_eval(C, a, idx, delta, outside, tmp):
   n=a.size
   s=1.0+delta
   if s <= 1e-14:
       return np.inf
   np.add(C[idx:idx+n], 2.0*delta*a, out=tmp)
   tmp[idx] += delta*delta
   m = tmp.max()
   val = (outside if outside > m else m) / (s*s)
   if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
   return val
```
But if `outside` is -inf and m finite, okay. If C has negative, m maybe negative? C nonnegative. Good.

`coord_update_one`:
Need constants:
```
_COORD_LOGFS = (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, -0.2, -0.05, 0.0, 0.05, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5)
_COORD_REFINE = (-0.04, -0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02, 0.04, -0.08, 0.08)
```

```
def coord_update_one(a, C, f, idx, pref, suf, tmp, add_scale, tol):
    n=a.size; L=C.size
    old=a[idx]
    left = pref[idx]
    right = suf[idx+n]
    outside = left if left>right else right
    best_val=f; best_delta=0.0
    if old > 1e-15:
        for lg in _COORD_LOGFS:
            if lg == 0.0: continue
            # For negative samples, ensure tau > 0
            tau = math.exp(lg)
            delta = old*(tau-1.0)
            if delta == 0.0: continue
            val = coord_candidate_eval(C,a,idx,delta,outside,tmp)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val=val; best_delta=delta
        if best_delta != 0.0 and best_val < f - 1e-16:
            best_lg = math.log(best_delta/old + 1.0)
            for h in _COORD_REFINE:
                lg = best_lg + h
                if lg < -20.0: continue
                tau = math.exp(lg)
                if tau <= 0.0: continue
                delta = old*(tau-1.0)
                if delta == 0.0: continue
                val = coord_candidate_eval(C,a,idx,delta,outside,tmp)
                if val < best_val:
                   best_val=val; best_delta=delta
    else:
        for factor in (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3):
            delta = add_scale*factor
            if delta <= 1e-18: continue
            val=...
    if best_val < f - tol:
        delta=best_delta
        C[idx:idx+n] += 2.0*delta*a
        C[2*idx] += delta*delta
        s=1.0+delta
        a[idx] = old + delta
        a *= (1.0/s)
        C *= (1.0/(s*s))
        # due to tiny neg
        # clip a? If old+delta negative? no
        return best_val, True
    return f, False
```

- In apply, `a *= 1/s` modifies all. But `a[idx]` changed old+delta, then scaled; correct. However if `a` array is used in `C[idx:idx+n] += ...` before modifying a; good.

`coord_polish`: Need handle `f <=0`: if all zeros? sequence sum1 so not.
```
def coord_polish(a, C, f, time_budget, top_k=None, focus=20.0, tol=1e-14, max_sweeps=500):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0)
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0: fallback
    a=a/s; C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    tmp=np.empty(len(a)); n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_f=f
    sweep=0
    while time<deadline and sweep<max_sweeps:
        sweep+=1
        # normalize current
        s=a.sum(); if s<=0: break
        a = np.maximum(a,0)
        a = a/s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = C.max()
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
        # gradient order
        if f <= 0: break
        mu=max(f/focus,1e-15)
        w=smooth_weights(C, mu)
        ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
        dot=np.dot(a,ga)
        gth=a*(ga-dot)
        if np.all(np.abs(gth)<1e-30): break
        order=np.argsort(-np.abs(gth))
        if top_k is not None and top_k < n:
            order=order[:top_k]
        pref,suf=make_pref_suf(C)
        add_scale=max(a.max(), 1e-6)
        improved=False
        for idx in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
            new_f, imp = coord_update_one(a,C,f,int(idx),pref,suf,tmp,add_scale,tol)
            if imp:
                improved=True; f=new_f
                pref,suf=make_pref_suf(C)
                add_scale=max(a.max(),1e-6)
                if f < best_f - tol:
                    best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
        # end sweep
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: break
        s=a.sum(); a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=s; C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
        elif f > best_f + 1e-12:
            # revert to best to avoid drift
            a=best_a.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); f=best_f
        if not improved:
            break
    a=best_a; s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s; C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
    return a,C,f
```
- At start of each sweep recompute C via FFT; this corrects. But if current a is best and f good, okay. 
- If no improvement in sweep, break. Could be coordinate local optimum.
- If time_budget small, max_sweeps.

Potential performance: `np.argsort(-np.abs(gth))` each sweep; gth length 4096. fine.

`run_adam_theta`: Need avoid too slow if time_budget 0.
```
def run_adam_theta(a, time_budget, focus_start=5.0, focus_end=300.0, lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.03, seed=7, restart_no_improve=800):
    if time_budget <=0: return a, f
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); a/=s
    n=a.size
    theta=a_to_theta(a)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max(); best_a=a.copy(); best_f=f
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+time_budget
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_imp=0
    while time<deadline:
       a=theta_to_a(theta)
       C=fft_conv_self(a)
       f=C.max()
       if not np.isfinite(f): break
       if f < best_f - 1e-15:
           best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); no_imp=0
       else:
           no_imp += 1
           if no_imp > restart_no_improve:
               th=a_to_theta(best_a)
               noise=0.02*(1.0 - (time.monotonic()-start)/time_budget) + 0.002
               theta=th + noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
               m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; no_imp=0
               # perhaps recompute a/C next loop
               continue
       progress=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start)/time_budget)
       if progress < 0.5:
          focus = focus_start + (focus_end*0.2 - focus_start)*(progress/0.5)
       else:
          focus = focus_end*0.2 + (focus_end - focus_end*0.2)*((progress-0.5)/0.5)
       mu=max(f/focus,1e-15)
       w=smooth_weights(C,mu)
       ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
       dot=np.dot(a,ga)
       gth=a*(ga-dot)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(gth)) or np.linalg.norm(gth) < 1e-30: break
       t += 1
       m = 0.9*m + 0.1*gth
       v = 0.999*v + 0.001*(gth*gth)
       mhat = m/(1-0.9**t)
       vhat = v/(1-0.999**t)
       lr = lr_end + (lr_start-lr_end)*(1.0-progress)
       theta = theta - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
       theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       theta -= theta.mean()
       # Optionally every 1000, recenter theta to best if no improve? already
    # final normalize
    a=best_a; C=fft_conv_self(a); f=C.max()
    return a,C,f
```
- `theta_to_a` each step uses exp full vector; okay. Could be many steps. If time 300s and each step ~0.001s? maybe 300k steps too many? Wait each step: fft_conv_self (1 FFT length8192), gradient (1 FFT length16384), softmax (exp4096). Maybe ~0.002-0.005s => 60k-150k in 300s. Each step modifies; 150k maybe enough. But Python loop overhead 0.0005s? plus numpy 2 FFT ~0.001? total maybe 2ms; 150s ->75k. okay.
- If `nrng standard_normal(n)` on restart alloc 4096, fine.
- If focus changes, mu changes; gradient consistent for Adam not required.

Potential problem: Adam in θ can drift and produce a with very small values; coordinate later can fix. Good.

`block_update_once`: Need top-level. Let's code carefully.
```
def block_update_once(a, C, f, tmpL, rng, K, focus=20.0, tol=1e-14):
    n=a.size; L=C.size
    if K<2 or f<=0: return a,C,f,False
    mu=max(f/focus,1e-15)
    w=smooth_weights(C, np.float64(mu)) # uses sym, okay for direction
    ga=gradient_weighted(w,a)
    dot=np.dot(a,ga)
    gth=a*(ga-dot)
    scores=np.abs(gth)
    # choose top K by scores
    if K < n:
       # use argpartition to avoid full sort? 
       part = np.argpartition(scores, K)[:K]
       # include a few random? maybe top K only
       idxs = part
    else: idxs=np.arange(n)
    vals = gth[idxs]
    maxv=np.max(np.abs(vals))
    if maxv <= 1e-18: return ...
    d=np.zeros(n)
    d[idxs] = -vals / maxv
    # center direction
    d -= np.mean(d)
    nz = np.nonzero(np.abs(d) > 1e-15)[0]
    if nz.size < 2: return
    maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
    if maxd <= 1e-18: return
    # t range
    pos = d > 1e-15; neg = d < -1e-15
    t_hi = np.inf
    if np.any(pos): t_hi = float(np.min(a[pos] / d[pos]))
    t_lo = -np.inf
    if np.any(neg): t_lo = float(np.max(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
    base = 0.03 / maxd
    # confine
    hi = min(t_hi, base*8.0)
    lo = max(t_lo, -base*8.0)
    if hi <= 1e-14 and lo >= -1e-14:
         # try very small
         hi=min(t_hi, base*1e-3)
         lo=max(t_lo, -base*1e-3)
    cands=[]
    if hi > 1e-14:
        for mult in (0.125,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0):
            t=base*mult
            if t <= hi: cands.append(t)
        if not cands: cands.append(min(hi, base))
    if lo < -1e-14:
        for mult in (0.125,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0):
            t=-base*mult
            if t >= lo: cands.append(t)
        if not cands: cands.append(max(lo, -base))
    if not cands: return
    # compute ad, dd
    ad=np.zeros(L)
    for j in nz:
        dj=d[j]
        if dj !=0:
             ad[j:j+n] += dj*a
    dd=np.zeros(L)
    # double loop
    for p in range(nz.size):
       jp=nz[p]; dj=d[jp]
       for q in range(nz.size):
          lq=nz[q]; jsum=jp+lq
          if jsum < L:
             dd[jsum] += dj*d[lq]
    sumd=float(np.sum(d))
    best_val=f; best_t=0.0
    for t in cands:
        denom=1.0 + t*sumd
        if denom <= 1e-12: continue
        np.add(C, 2.0*t*ad, out=tmpL)
        tmpL += t*t*dd
        m=np.max(tmpL)
        val=m/(denom*denom)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val=val; best_t=t
    if best_t != 0.0 and best_val < f - 1e-15:
        # refine around best_t
        for rel in (-0.5,-0.25,-0.1,0.1,0.25,0.5):
            t2=best_t*(1.0+rel)
            # ensure within range and sign? If t2 crosses 0, allow if nonzero
            if (best_t >0 and (t2 < 0 or t2 > t_hi)): continue
            if (best_t <0 and (t2 > 0 or t2 < t_lo)): continue
            denom=1+t2*sumd
            if denom <=1e-12: continue
            np.add(C, 2*t2*ad, out=tmpL); tmpL += t2*t2*dd
            val=np.max(tmpL)/(denom*denom)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val=val; best_t=t2
    if best_val < f - tol:
        # safe apply
        a_old=a.copy(); C_old=C.copy()
        a += best_t*d
        ss=1.0+best_t*sumd
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            return a_old,C_old,f,False
        C += 2*best_t*ad + best_t*best_t*dd
        a /= ss
        C /= (ss*ss)
        a = np.maximum(a,0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s <= 1e-15:
            return a_old,C_old,f,False
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f_rec = C.max()
        if f_rec <= f + tol: # if not worse, accept
            return a,C,f_rec,True
        else:
            return a_old,C_old,f,False
    return a,C,f,False
```
- `smooth_weights` sym okay for direction only. Value/gradient not exact. Good.
- Direct ad loop: `ad[j:j+n] += dj*a`; if j+n may exceed L? j<n, j+n<=n+n=2n, L=2n-1; for j=n-1, slice length n? j+n =2n-1, okay `ad[n-1:2n-1]` n elements. Good.
- dd double loop: K maybe up to 64; K² 4096, okay. Use `d[lq]` fetch.
- `tmpL` length L; ensure passed. In block_polish, create `tmpL=np.empty(L)`.
- In apply, `C += 2*...` modifies C; if f_rec worse, restore. Good.
- Centering d subtract mean may change sumd not zero exactly due to mean of d over all n; if d sparse, mean small. Fine.

`block_polish`:
```
def block_polish(a,C,f,time_budget,seed=11,K_list=(8,16,32),tol=1e-14):
   a=np.maximum...; C fft maybe; best...
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; tmpL=np.empty(L); rng=default_rng(seed)
   start=deadline; count=0; fail=0; max_fails=6
   while time<deadline:
      if fail >= max_fails:
         # noise restart
         theta=a_to_theta(best_a?) use current best.
         noise=0.002 + 0.005*(fail/max_fails)
         theta += noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
         a=theta_to_a(theta); C=fft; f=C.max()
         if f < best_f: update
         fail=0; max_fails += 2
         if time > deadline - 30: break
      K=K_list[count % len(K_list)]; count+=1
      # use current a,C,f; if current worse than best, reset to best
      if f > best_f + 1e-12:
          a=best_a.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); f=best_f
      a2,C2,f2,imp = block_update_once(a,C,f,tmpL,rng,K)
      if imp:
         a,C,f=a2,C2,f2
         if f < best_f - tol:
             best_f=f; best_a=a.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
         fail=0
      else:
         fail += 1
   # return best
```
- If block no improve many times, noise. Good.

Now, `sqrt_root_init` with vectorized dot. Need if L=2n-1. `target=1/L`.
```
def sqrt_root_init(n):
   L=2*n-1
   a=np.zeros(n)
   a[0]=1.0/np.sqrt(L)
   t=1.0/L
   for k in range(1,n):
       if k-1 >=0:
           s=float(np.dot(a[:k], a[k-1::-1]))
       else: s=0
       b=2.0*s
       disc=b*b + t
       a[k]=0.5*(-b + np.sqrt(disc))
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
   return a
```
Will this be slow if n large? okay. For n=4096, 4095 dot calls. Use Python loop; maybe 0.1-0.5s. Good. If disc tiny negative? use max(0). `np.sqrt(max(disc,0.0))`.

`cosine_init`:
```
def cosine_init(n, rng):
   i=np.arange(n)
   a=np.ones(n)
   for _ in range(12):
       fnp=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//4)))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi))
       a += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*fnp*i/n + ph)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.05)
   a/=a.sum()
   return a
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=max(1.0, min(float(budget_s), 2350.0))
    deadline=start+total_budget-30
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+0)
    parent_raw=None
    try:
       hs=height_sequence_1
       arr=np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if arr.size>=64 and arr.sum()>0.01:
           parent_raw=arr
    except Exception: parent_raw=None
    if parent_raw is not None and 512 <= parent_raw.size <= 8192:
        n=parent_raw.size
    else:
        n=4096
    # build candidates
    candidates=[]
    if parent_raw is not None:
        a=parent_raw.copy(); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
        candidates.append(('parent', a))
    # roots
    if 1 <= n <= 8192 and time<deadline-60:
       root=sqrt_root_init(n)
       candidates.append(('root',root)); candidates.append(('root_rev',root[::-1]));

       candidates.append(('cos'+str(r),a))

    # uniform

       best_a,best_C,best_f=coord_polish(best_a,best_C,best_f, time_alloc, focus=20)

    # main loop with methods

Maybe add **peak-specific pair moves**: For each active lag k, C_k=Σ_i a_i a_{k-i}. To reduce C_k, transfer mass from a pair (i,j) with i+j=k. Pair swap: increase one, decrease other. It can reduce pair product without changing sum. More precise than scaling. Let's consider implementing a **pair transfer move** for coordinates involved in active peaks. This could be very effective.

Pair transfer: For i != j, choose δ such that a_i' = a_i + δ, a_j' = a_j - δ, others same; sum remains 1. Nonneg: δ ∈ [-a_i, a_j]. F(δ) = max_k (C_k + δ^2? formula). Let's derive.
Modify a_i += δ, a_j -= δ (if i,j distinct). C_{new} = C + δ terms for i and -δ for j. Increment = 2δ (u_i - u_j) - δ²(e_{2i}+e_{2j}) + 2δ² e_{i+j}? Because cross term between i and j changes? Let's derive: a_i' = a_i+δ, a_j'=a_j-δ. Original terms involving i,j:
- i,i: +2a_iδ+δ²
- j,j: -2a_jδ+δ²
- i,j and j,i for lag i+j: original 2 a_i a_j (if i≠j), new 2(a_i+δ)(a_j-δ)=2a_i a_j +2δ(a_j-a_i) -2δ². Increment = 2δ(a_j-a_i) -2δ².
Other k: from i: 2δ a_{k-i}; from j: -2δ a_{k-j}. So total increment for all k: 2δ (a_{k-i} - a_{k-j}) + δ²(δ_{k,2i}+δ_{k,2j}) + [if k=i+j and i≠j] -2δ². This can be evaluated O(n) using two shifted a arrays. Denominator 1 (sum unchanged). This move exactly redistributes mass without global scaling. It might be superior to individual scaling because it preserves sum and can cancel pair product. We can implement line search over δ for selected pairs. This could improve local optimum significantly.

def pair_precompute_pair(a,C,i,j,n,L):
    d1=np.zeros(L); p=np.zeros(L)
    d1[i:i+n] += a
    d1[j:j+n] -= a
    p[2*i] += 1
    p[2*j] += 1
    s=i+j
    if s < L: p[s] -= 2
    return d1,p
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

The implementation combines:
  1. Internal FFT-based model of the true evaluator objective.
  2. Exact one-coordinate scaling line searches on the normalized simplex.
  3. Exact two-coordinate mass-transfer line searches.
  4. Low-dimensional block gradient line searches.
  5. Smooth log-sum-exp gradient methods (Adam and L-BFGS-B when available).
  6. Adaptive quality verification and safe fallbacks.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAVE_LBFGS = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LBFGS = False

# Global state for L-BFGS callbacks (no closures/lambdas).
_LB = {
    "mu": None,
    "cache_theta": None,
    "cache_val": 0.0,
    "cache_grad": None,
}

_COORD_LOGFS = (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, -0.2, -0.05, 0.0, 0.05, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5)
_COORD_REFINE = (-0.08, -0.04, -0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02, 0.04, 0.08)
_PAIR_FRACS = (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 0.95, 1.0)
_PAIR_REFINE = (-0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)


def theta_to_a(theta):
    """Convert log-soft-pm parameters to a nonnegative unit-sum vector."""
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if th.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        th = np.nan_to_num(th, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    th = th - np.max(th)
    e = np.exp(th)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(th.shape, 1.0 / th.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def a_to_theta(a):
    """Convert a nonnegative sequence to centered log proportions."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    th = np.log(a)
    th -= th.mean()
    return th


def fft_conv_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:size]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def direct_conv_self(a):
    """Direct self-convolution, used sparingly for final verification."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)


def gradient_weighted(W, a):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k W[k] * conv(a,a)[k] with respect to a.
    W is length 2n-1, a is length n.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    W = np.asarray(W, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = W.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    ar = a[::-1]
    N = L + n - 1
    fsize = 1 << (N - 1).bit_length()
    conv = np.real(ifft(fft(W, fsize) * fft(ar, fsize)))
    g = conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return 2.0 * g


def smooth_weights(C, mu):
    """Softmax weights over convolution coefficients, regularized by mu."""
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    L = C.size
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return w
    f = float(C.max())
    if mu <= 1e-300 or f <= 1e-300:
        w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return w
    z = C / float(mu)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    z = z - zmax
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w[:] = 0.0
        w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        s2 = float(w.sum())
        if s2 > 1e-300:
            w /= s2
    return w


def make_pref_suf(C):
    """Prefix and suffix maxima for O(1) max outside an interval."""
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    L = C.size
    pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = -np.inf
    if L:
        pref[1:] = np.maximum.accumulate(C)
    suf = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    suf[L] = -np.inf
    if L:
        rmax = np.maximum.accumulate(C[::-1])
        suf[:L] = rmax[::-1]
    return pref, suf


def coord_candidate_eval(C, a, idx, delta, outside, tmp):
    """Evaluate exact one-coordinate scaling candidate."""
    n = a.size
    s = 1.0 + delta
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.inf
    np.add(C[idx:idx + n], 2.0 * delta * a, out=tmp)
    tmp[idx] += delta * delta
    m = float(tmp.max())
    val = (outside if outside > m else m) / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def coord_update_one(a, C, f, idx, pref, suf, tmp, add_scale, tol):
    """
    Exact one-coordinate scaling/mass-creation move.
    Returns (new_f, improved).
    """
    n = a.size
    old = float(a[idx])
    left = pref[idx]
    right = suf[idx + n]
    outside = left if left > right else right
    best_val = f
    best_delta = 0.0

    if old > 1e-15:
        for lg in _COORD_LOGFS:
            if lg == 0.0:
                continue
            tau = math.exp(lg)
            delta = old * (tau - 1.0)
            if delta == 0.0:
                continue
            val = coord_candidate_eval(C, a, idx, delta, outside, tmp)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_delta = delta

        if best_delta != 0.0 and best_val < f - 1e-16:
            best_lg = math.log(best_delta / old + 1.0)
            for h in _COORD_REFINE:
                lg = best_lg + h
                if lg < -20.0:
                    continue
                tau = math.exp(lg)
                if tau <= 0.0:
                    continue
                delta = old * (tau - 1.0)
                if delta == 0.0:
                    continue
                val = coord_candidate_eval(C, a, idx, delta, outside, tmp)
                if val < best_val:
                    best_val = val
                    best_delta = delta
    else:
        for factor in (0.02, 0.08, 0.25, 0.8):
            delta = float(add_scale) * factor
            if delta <= 1e-18:
                continue
            val = coord_candidate_eval(C, a, idx, delta, outside, tmp)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_delta = delta

    if best_val < f - tol:
        delta = float(best_delta)
        C[idx:idx + n] += 2.0 * delta * a
        C[2 * idx] += delta * delta
        s = 1.0 + delta
        if s <= 1e-14:
            return f, False
        a[idx] = old + delta
        a *= 1.0 / s
        C *= 1.0 / (s * s)
        return best_val, True

    return f, False


def coord_polish(a, C, f, time_budget, focus=30.0, top_k=None, tol=1e-14):
    """
    Repeated exact coordinate-scaling line searches.
    This is often the strongest local polish.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    if time_budget <= 0:
        return a, C, f

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a /= s
    C = fft_conv_self(a)
    f = float(C.max())

    tmp = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_budget)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_f = f
    sweep = 0
    max_sweeps = 1000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and sweep < max_sweeps:
        sweep += 1
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()

        if f <= 1e-300:
            break

        mu = max(f / float(focus), 1e-15)
        W = smooth_weights(C, mu)
        ga = gradient_weighted(W, a)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        gth = a * (ga - dot)

        scores = np.abs(gth)
        if not np.any(scores > 1e-30):
            break

        order = np.argsort(-scores)
        if top_k is not None and top_k < n:
            order = order[:top_k]

        pref, suf = make_pref_suf(C)
        add_scale = max(float(a.max()), 1e-6)
        improved = False

        for idx in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.7:
                break
            idx = int(idx)
            new_f, imp = coord_update_one(a, C, f, idx, pref, suf, tmp, add_scale, tol)
            if imp:
                improved = True
                f = new_f
                pref, suf = make_pref_suf(C)
                add_scale = max(float(a.max()), 1e-6)
                if f < best_f - tol:
                    best_f = f
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_C = C.copy()

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
            break

        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()
        elif f > best_f + 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            f = best_f

        if not improved:
            break

    a = best_a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
    return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())


def pair_polish(a, C, f, time_budget, seed=101, top_k=20, active_m=8,
                max_pairs=350, tol=1e-14):
    """
    Exact two-coordinate mass-transfer line searches.
    Moves mass between pairs of coordinates that contribute to high peaks.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_budget <= 0:
        return a, C, f

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a /= s
    C = fft_conv_self(a)
    f = float(C.max())
    L = 2 * n - 1

    tmpL = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)
    d1 = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    pp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_budget)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_f = f
    pass_no = 0
    max_passes = 300

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and pass_no < max_passes:
        pass_no += 1
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()

        if f <= 1e-300:
            break

        mu = max(f / 25.0, 1e-15)
        W = smooth_weights(C, mu)
        ga = gradient_weighted(W, a)
        dotg = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        gth = a * (ga - dotg)

        order_g = np.argsort(-np.abs(gth))[:top_k]
        order_a = np.argsort(-a)[:top_k]
        top = []
        seen_top = set()
        for ii in list(order_g) + list(order_a):
            ii = int(ii)
            if ii not in seen_top:
                seen_top.add(ii)
                top.append(ii)
        if not top:
            break

        active = np.argsort(-C)[:max(1, active_m)]
        pair_set = set()

        # Pairs induced by current strongest peaks.
        for k in active:
            k = int(k)
            for ii in top[:16]:
                jj = k - int(ii)
                if 0 <= jj < n and jj != ii:
                    pair_set.add((min(int(ii), jj), max(int(ii), jj)))

        # Pairs among the strongest coordinates.
        tt = top[:14]
        for u in range(len(tt)):
            for v in range(u + 1, len(tt)):
                pair_set.add((min(int(tt[u]), int(tt[v])), max(int(tt[u]), int(tt[v]))))

        if not pair_set:
            break

        p_list = list(pair_set)
        if len(p_list) > max_pairs:
            arr = np.empty((len(p_list), 2), dtype=np.int64)
            for r, pr in enumerate(p_list):
                arr[r, 0] = pr[0]
                arr[r, 1] = pr[1]
            pot = a[arr[:, 0]] * a[arr[:, 1]]
            o = np.argsort(-pot)
            arr = arr[o[:max_pairs]]
            p_list = []
            for r in range(arr.shape[0]):
                p_list.append((int(arr[r, 0]), int(arr[r, 1])))

        improved_pass = False

        for (ii, jj) in p_list:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            ai = float(a[ii])
            aj = float(a[jj])
            dmax = max(ai, aj)
            if dmax <= 1e-16:
                continue

            # precompute delta-linear and delta^2 coefficient vectors
            d1.fill(0.0)
            pp.fill(0.0)
            d1[ii:ii + n] += 2.0 * a
            d1[jj:jj + n] -= 2.0 * a
            pp[2 * ii] += 1.0
            pp[2 * jj] += 1.0
            ssum = ii + jj
            if ssum < L:
                pp[ssum] -= 2.0

            best_delta = 0.0
            best_val = f
            deltas = [0.0]
            for frac in _PAIR_FRACS:
                pos = frac * dmax
                neg = -frac * dmax
                if pos <= aj and pos >= -ai:
                    deltas.append(float(pos))
                if neg >= -ai and neg <= aj:
                    deltas.append(float(neg))

            for dq in deltas:
                if dq == 0.0:
                    continue
                dq = max(-ai, min(aj, dq))
                np.add(C, dq * d1, out=tmpL)
                tmpL += dq * dq * pp
                val = float(tmpL.max())
                if val < best_val:
                    best_val = val
                    best_delta = dq

            if best_delta != 0.0 and best_val < f - 1e-16:
                base = max(abs(best_delta) * 0.35, dmax * 0.015)
                for rel in _PAIR_REFINE:
                    dq = best_delta + rel * base
                    dq = max(-ai, min(aj, float(dq)))
                    if dq == 0.0:
                        continue
                    np.add(C, dq * d1, out=tmpL)
                    tmpL += dq * dq * pp
                    val = float(tmpL.max())
                    if val < best_val:
                        best_val = val
                        best_delta = dq

            if best_val < f - tol:
                dq = float(best_delta)
                a[ii] += dq
                a[jj] -= dq
                nd = dq * dq
                C += dq * d1
                C += nd * pp
                if a[ii] < -1e-12 or a[jj] < -1e-12:
                    # Rare numerical protection; recompute from scratch.
                    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                    ss = float(a.sum())
                    if ss <= 1e-300:
                        break
                    a /= ss
                    C = fft_conv_self(a)
                    f = float(C.max())
                else:
                    f = best_val
                if f < best_f - tol:
                    best_f = f
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_C = C.copy()
                improved_pass = True

        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f - tol:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()
        elif f > best_f + 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            f = best_f

        if not improved_pass:
            break

    a = best_a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
    return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())


def block_candidate_value(t, C, ad, dd, sumd, tmpL):
    """Evaluate a block perturbation t along sparse direction d."""
    denom = 1.0 + t * sumd
    if denom <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    np.add(C, 2.0 * t * ad, out=tmpL)
    tmpL += t * t * dd
    val = float(tmpL.max()) / (denom * denom)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def block_update_once(a, C, f, tmpL, rng, K, focus=20.0, tol=1e-14):
    """
    One low-dimensional block line search along a combined gradient direction.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = C.size
    if n == 0 or K < 2 or f <= 1e-300:
        return a, C, f, False

    mu = max(f / float(focus), 1e-15)
    W = smooth_weights(C, mu)
    ga = gradient_weighted(W, a)
    dotg = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gth = a * (ga - dotg)
    scores = np.abs(gth)
    if K < n:
        idxs = np.argpartition(scores, K)[:K]
    else:
        idxs = np.arange(n)

    vals = gth[idxs]
    maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(vals)))
    if maxv <= 1e-18:
        return a, C, f, False

    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d[idxs] = -vals / maxv
    d -= float(np.mean(d))
    nz = np.nonzero(np.abs(d) > 1e-15)[0]
    if nz.size < 2:
        return a, C, f, False

    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxd <= 1e-18:
        return a, C, f, False

    # Feasible t range for a + t d >= 0.
    t_low = -np.inf
    pos = d > 1e-15
    if np.any(pos):
        t_low = float(np.max(-a[pos] / d[pos]))
    t_high = np.inf
    neg = d < -1e-15
    if np.any(neg):
        t_high = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))

    base = 0.03 / maxd
    lo = max(t_low, -base * 8.0, -1e-12)
    hi = min(t_high, base * 8.0, 1e-12)
    if lo > 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    if hi < 0.0:
        hi = 0.0

    cands = []
    if hi > 1e-14:
        for mult in (0.0625, 0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
            t = base * mult
            if t <= hi:
                cands.append(float(t))
        if not cands:
            cands.append(float(min(hi, base)))
    if lo < -1e-14:
        for mult in (0.0625, 0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
            t = -base * mult
            if t >= lo:
                cands.append(float(t))
        if not cands:
            cands.append(float(max(lo, -base)))
    if not cands:
        return a, C, f, False

    ad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in nz:
        dj = float(d[j])
        if dj != 0.0:
            ad[j:j + n] += dj * a

    dd = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    m = nz.size
    for p in range(m):
        jp = int(nz[p])
        dj = float(d[jp])
        if dj == 0.0:
            continue
        for q in range(m):
            lq = int(nz[q])
            ssum = jp + lq
            if ssum < L:
                dd[ssum] += dj * float(d[lq])

    sumd = float(np.sum(d))
    best_val = f
    best_t = 0.0
    for t in cands:
        val = block_candidate_value(t, C, ad, dd, sumd, tmpL)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_t = t

    if best_t != 0.0 and best_val < f - 1e-15:
        for rel in (-0.5, -0.25, -0.1, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
            t2 = best_t * (1.0 + rel)
            if t2 > 0.0:
                if t2 > hi or t2 < 0.0:
                    continue
            elif t2 < 0.0:
                if t2 < lo or t2 > 0.0:
                    continue
            else:
                continue
            val = block_candidate_value(t2, C, ad, dd, sumd, tmpL)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_t = t2

    if best_val < f - tol:
        a_old = a.copy()
        C_old = C.copy()
        a = a.copy()
        C = C.copy()
        a += best_t * d
        ss = 1.0 + best_t * sumd
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            return a_old, C_old, f, False
        C += 2.0 * best_t * ad
        C += best_t * best_t * dd
        a /= ss
        C /= (ss * ss)

        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        ss = float(a.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-15:
            return a_old, C_old, f, False
        a /= ss
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        fr = float(C.max())
        if fr <= f + tol:
            return a, C, fr, True
        return a_old, C_old, f, False

    return a, C, f, False


def block_polish(a, C, f, time_budget, seed=202, k_choice=(8, 16, 32), tol=1e-14):
    """Iterate low-dimensional block line searches with small perturbations."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_budget <= 0:
        return a, C, f

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a /= s
    C = fft_conv_self(a)
    f = float(C.max())
    L = 2 * n - 1
    tmpL = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_budget)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_f = f
    count = 0
    fails = 0
    max_fails = 4

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if f > best_f + 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            f = best_f

        if fails >= max_fails:
            base = best_a if f > best_f + 1e-12 else a
            th = a_to_theta(base)
            noise = 0.003 + 0.008 * (fails / max_fails)
            th = th + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
            a = theta_to_a(th)
            C = fft_conv_self(a)
            f = float(C.max())
            if f < best_f - tol:
                best_f = f
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_C = C.copy()
            fails = 0
            max_fails += 2
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 25:
                break

        K = int(k_choice[count % len(k_choice)])
        count += 1
        a2, C2, f2, imp = block_update_once(a, C, f, tmpL, rng, K)
        if imp:
            a, C, f = a2, C2, f2
            fails = 0
            if f < best_f - tol:
                best_f = f
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_C = C.copy()
        else:
            fails += 1

    a = best_a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
    return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())


def run_adam_theta(a, time_budget, focus_start=5.0, focus_end=300.0,
                   lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.025, seed=7, restart_no_improve=900):
    """
    Adam on log-soft-pm coordinates using a reweighted smooth max surrogate.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_budget <= 0:
        return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a /= s
    C = fft_conv_self(a)
    f = float(C.max())

    theta = a_to_theta(a)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = f
    no_imp = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_budget)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = theta_to_a(theta)
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            break
        if f < best_f - 1e-15:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > restart_no_improve:
                th2 = a_to_theta(best_a)
                progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / float(time_budget))
                noise = 0.025 * max(0.25, 1.0 - progress) + 0.002
                theta = th2 + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
                theta -= theta.mean()
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                no_imp = 0
                continue

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / float(time_budget))
        if progress < 0.5:
            focus = focus_start + (max(focus_start + 15.0, focus_end * 0.2) - focus_start) * (progress / 0.5)
        else:
            focus = max(focus_start + 15.0, focus_end * 0.2) + (focus_end - max(focus_start + 15.0, focus_end * 0.2)) * ((progress - 0.5) / 0.5)

        mu = max(f / float(focus), 1e-15)
        W = smooth_weights(C, mu)
        ga = gradient_weighted(W, a)
        dotg = float(np.dot(a, ga))
        gth = a * (ga - dotg)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gth)) or float(np.linalg.norm(gth)) < 1e-30:
            break

        t += 1
        b1, b2 = 0.9, 0.999
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gth
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gth * gth)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        lr = lr_end + (lr_start - lr_end) * (1.0 - progress)
        theta = theta - lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
        theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        theta -= theta.mean()

    a = best_a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f:
            best_f = f
    return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())


def _lb_compute(theta):
    """Value and gradient for fixed-mu smooth log-sum-exp objective."""
    mu = _LB["mu"]
    n = int(np.asarray(theta).size)
    if mu is None or mu <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        th = np.nan_to_num(th, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    th = th - float(np.mean(th))

    ct = _LB["cache_theta"]
    if ct is not None and ct.shape == th.shape and np.array_equal(ct, th):
        return float(_LB["cache_val"]), np.asarray(_LB["cache_grad"], dtype=np.float64)

    a = theta_to_a(th)
    C = fft_conv_self(a)
    L = C.size
    f = float(C.max())
    if f <= 1e-300 or L == 0:
        res = (0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
        return res

    z = C / float(mu)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    z = z - zmax
    w = np.exp(z)
    ss = float(w.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= ss

    ga = gradient_weighted(w, a)
    dotg = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gth = a * (ga - dotg)
    gth = np.nan_to_num(gth, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    val = float(mu * (zmax + math.log(max(ss, 1e-300))))
    _LB["cache_theta"] = th.copy()
    _LB["cache_val"] = val
    _LB["cache_grad"] = gth.copy()
    return val, gth


def _lb_val(theta):
    val, _ = _lb_compute(theta)
    return float(val)


def _lb_grad(theta):
    _, g = _lb_compute(theta)
    return g


def run_lbfags(a, mu, maxfun=1000, maxiter=600):
    """
    L-BFGS-B on smooth log-sum-exp objective with fixed mu.
    Returns the best of initial/final points.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, fft_conv_self(a), float(fft_conv_self(a).max())

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    a /= s
    C0 = fft_conv_self(a)
    f0 = float(C0.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_f = f0

    if not _HAVE_LBFGS:
        return best_a, fft_conv_self(best_a), best_f

    theta = a_to_theta(a)
    theta -= theta.mean()

    _LB["mu"] = float(mu)
    _LB["cache_theta"] = None

    try:
        res = _scipy_minimize(
            _lb_val,
            theta,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=_lb_grad,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "maxls": 30,
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-12,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            a2 = theta_to_a(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
            a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
            s2 = float(a2.sum())
            if s2 > 1e-300:
                a2 /= s2
                C2 = fft_conv_self(a2)
                f2 = float(C2.max())
                if f2 < best_f:
                    best_f = f2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    _LB["mu"] = None
    _LB["cache_theta"] = None

    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        best_a /= s
        Cb = fft_conv_self(best_a)
        fb = float(Cb.max())
        if fb < best_f:
            best_f = fb
    return best_a, fft_conv_self(best_a), float(fft_conv_self(best_a).max())


def sqrt_root_init(n):
    """
    Causal polynomial square root of the uniform convolution target.
    A useful structured nonnegative initializer.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(L))
    target = 1.0 / float(L)
    for k in range(1, n):
        s = float(np.dot(a[:k], a[k - 1::-1]))
        b = 2.0 * s
        disc = b * b + target
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        a[k] = 0.5 * (-b + math.sqrt(disc))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
    return a


def cosine_init(n, seed):
    """Random low-amplitude cosine mix, clipped to nonnegative."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(14):
        fmax = max(2, n // 4)
        f = int(rng.integers(1, fmax + 1))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.06, 0.38))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * i / float(n) + ph)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.05)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        a /= s
    return a


def make_initial_candidates(n, parent_arr, seed):
    """Create a small pool of diversified initial sequences."""
    candidates = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        a = np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if a.size == n and s > 1e-12:
            a /= s
            candidates.append(("parent", a))

    if n <= 8192:
        root = sqrt_root_init(n)
        candidates.append(("root", root))
        rv = root[::-1].copy()
        candidates.append(("root_rev", rv))
        rs = 0.5 * (root + root[::-1])
        rs = np.maximum(rs, 1e-8)
        s = float(rs.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            rs /= s
            candidates.append(("root_sym", rs))

    rngs = [seed + 911, seed + 1823, seed + 2729, seed + 3631]
    for ii, sd in enumerate(rngs):
        c = cosine_init(n, sd)
        candidates.append(("cos%d" % ii, c))

    u = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    candidates.append(("uniform", u))

    return candidates


def safe_evaluate(seq):
    """Call evaluator if available, otherwise return internal objective."""
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        try:
            a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
            n = a.size
            s = float(a.sum())
            if n == 0 or s <= 1e-300:
                return float("inf")
            C = direct_conv_self(a)
            return float(2.0 * n * float(C.max()) / (s * s))
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    """Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the given objective."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    raw_budget = float(budget_s)
    total_budget = max(90.0, min(raw_budget, 2350.0))
    deadline = start + total_budget - 35.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size >= 64 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.01:
            parent_arr = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None and 512 <= parent_arr.size <= 8192:
        n = int(parent_arr.size)
    else:
        n = 4096

    candidates = make_initial_candidates(n, parent_arr, seed)
    best_a = None
    best_C = None
    best_f = float("inf")

    for name, cand in candidates:
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if cand.size != n:
            continue
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            continue
        cand /= s
        Cc = fft_conv_self(cand)
        fc = float(Cc.max())
        score = 2.0 * n * fc
        print("init %-10s n=%d internal_score=%.7f" % (name, n, score))
        if fc < best_f:
            best_f = fc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            best_C = Cc.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_C = fft_conv_self(best_a)
        best_f = float(best_C.max())
        print("fallback uniform n=%d internal_score=%.7f" % (n, 2.0 * n * best_f))

    def update_best(a, C, f):
        nonlocal best_a, best_C, best_f
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return False
        a /= s
        C = fft_conv_self(a)
        f = float(C.max())
        if f < best_f - 1e-15:
            best_f = f
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()
            return True
        return False

    # Phase 0: quick coordinate polish to exploit the loaded construction.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 70:
        alloc = min(180.0, max(25.0, 0.10 * total_budget))
        a, C, f = coord_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, focus=25.0)
        update_best(a, C, f)
        print("P0 coord score=%.7f time=%.1f" % (2.0 * n * best_f, time.monotonic() - start))

    # Phase 1: mixed local/polish loop.
    last_imp = 0
    cycle = 0
    lbf_focuses = [10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 320.0, 1000.0]
    lbf_idx = 0

    while deadline - time.monotonic() > 320.0:
        cycle += 1
        old_f = best_f

        # coordinate polish
        if deadline - time.monotonic() > 150.0:
            alloc = min(300.0, max(40.0, 0.12 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
            a, C, f = coord_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, focus=30.0)
            update_best(a, C, f)

        # pair mass-transfer polish
        if deadline - time.monotonic() > 150.0:
            alloc = min(110.0, max(30.0, 0.05 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
            a, C, f = pair_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 700 + cycle)
            update_best(a, C, f)

        improved_this_cycle = best_f < old_f - 1e-15
        if improved_this_cycle:
            last_imp = cycle
            print("cycle=%d improved score=%.7f time=%.1f" % (cycle, 2.0 * n * best_f, time.monotonic() - start))

        if deadline - time.monotonic() > 220.0 and (cycle - last_imp >= 2 or cycle % 3 == 0):
            if _HAVE_LBFGS:
                foc = lbf_focuses[lbf_idx % len(lbf_focuses)]
                lbf_idx += 1
                mu = max(best_f / foc, 1e-18)
                maxfun = 1400 if foc <= 30.0 else 900
                maxiter = 700 if foc <= 30.0 else 450
                a, C, f = run_lbfags(
                    best_a,
                    mu=mu,
                    maxfun=maxfun,
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                )
                update_best(a, C, f)
            else:
                alloc = min(130.0, max(40.0, 0.06 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
                a, C, f = run_adam_theta(
                    best_a,
                    alloc,
                    focus_start=8.0,
                    focus_end=400.0,
                    seed=seed + 500 + cycle,
                )
                update_best(a, C, f)

            if deadline - time.monotonic() > 100.0:
                alloc = min(80.0, max(25.0, 0.03 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
                a, C, f = coord_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, focus=40.0)
                update_best(a, C, f)

            if deadline - time.monotonic() > 90.0:
                alloc = min(60.0, max(20.0, 0.02 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
                a, C, f = pair_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 900 + cycle)
                update_best(a, C, f)

    # Phase 2: block and pair exploitation if time remains.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 180.0:
        alloc = min(220.0, max(40.0, 0.08 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        a, C, f = block_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 1111)
        update_best(a, C, f)

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 120.0:
        alloc = min(160.0, max(30.0, 0.05 * (deadline - time.monotonic())))
        a, C, f = pair_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 1222, top_k=24, max_pairs=500)
        update_best(a, C, f)

    # Phase 3: Adam escape if there is room.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 260.0:
        alloc = min(260.0, 0.12 * (deadline - time.monotonic()))
        a, C, f = run_adam_theta(
            best_a,
            alloc,
            focus_start=20.0,
            focus_end=1200.0,
            lr_start=0.07,
            lr_end=0.012,
            seed=seed + 3000,
            restart_no_improve=650,
        )
        update_best(a, C, f)

    # Final extensive coordinate polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 50.0:
        alloc = max(10.0, remaining - 20.0)
        a, C, f = coord_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, focus=45.0)
        update_best(a, C, f)

    # Final pair polish if still possible.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 40.0:
        alloc = max(8.0, remaining - 12.0)
        a, C, f = pair_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 4000, max_pairs=700)
        update_best(a, C, f)

    # Optional tiny block finish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 35.0:
        alloc = max(5.0, remaining - 8.0)
        a, C, f = block_polish(best_a, best_C, best_f, alloc, seed=seed + 5000)
        update_best(a, C, f)

    # Final normalization and direct objective check.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.size and best_a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        best_a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    best_a /= s

    Cd = direct_conv_self(best_a)
    fd = float(Cd.max())
    internal_score = 2.0 * n * fd
    print("final_internal_direct_score=%.7f" % internal_score)

    out_list = [float(x) for x in best_a.tolist()]

    try:
        ver = safe_evaluate(out_list)
        print("final_evaluator_score=%.7f" % ver)
        if not np.isfinite(ver):
            raise ValueError("invalid evaluator result")
    except Exception as e:
        print("evaluator check failed: %s" % str(e))
        out_list = [1.0 / 256.0] * 256

    # Absolute safety fallback for malformed output.
    if not out_list:
        out_list = [1.0 / 256.0] * 256

    try:
        for x in out_list:
            if isinstance(x, bool) or not isinstance(x, (int, float)):
                raise ValueError
            if math.isnan(x) or math.isinf(x):
                raise ValueError
            if x < 0.0:
                raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        out_list = [1.0 / 256.0] * 256

    return out_list
```
